L78 · CustomRolesrendered textCustom Roles
L78 · CustomRolesUI attributeNamed variations of a base role for specific staff. A custom role starts from its base role and can only remove actions; it never grants access beyond the base role and never crosses clinic ownership. Assign it to staff who already have that base role. Leave the clinic empty to apply it in all of that person's clinics. When several rules apply, the most restrictive wins, and a restricted action whose clinic is unclear requires a clinic to be chosen.

L118 · SmsProviderPanelUI attributeAbout the Keep in Touch request
L118 · SmsProviderPanelUI attributeUses POST with application/json and Bearer authorization. The body contains countryCode, mobile, senderId, message, apiKey, clientId and templateId. Entity ID is not sent. Publish each event’s DLT Template ID in Notifications. Its URL is separate from the retained Custom API URL and mapping. HTTP 200 with boolean Success:true (or lowercase success) means acceptance; Success:false is rejection. Malformed or conflicting responses remain uncertain. Requests wait up to 30 seconds.
